Across three scenarios (optimistic, moderate, pessimistic), the optimal alpha* is interior or at the rules-only boundary and shifts weakly toward character shaping as deployment scale T grows, from negligible (Delta alpha* = +0.01) to pronounced (Delta alpha* = +0.21) depending on scenario. The dominant parameter is the baseline character fragility rate p^(0)_frag, which shifts alpha* by 0.50 across its range -- far exceeding the effect of tail severity, filter quality, or common-mode failure probability. CVaR and expected-harm optima converge at large T. These results suggest that safety architecture decisions depend less on deployment scale per se than on the reliability of character shaping under distributional shift.

</details>


### [237] [TopoIntent: Compiling Security Intent into Executable, Compliance-Checked Network Topologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13389)
*Xiaokang Qu,Jianliang Ma,Zao Fan,Tianshu Chu,Tianlong Fan,Linyuan Lü*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TopoIntent 是一个将自然语言安全意图自动编译为可执行、合规校验的企业安全拓扑系统，通过模板检索、分段融合和结构化修复实现，在自建评测集上显著提升了合规满足率与策略通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业安全拓扑设计需要将业务意图、合规要求与风险假设转化为区域、边界设备、区间路径和访问控制策略，但现有 NetOps 自动化工具只在拓扑固定后操作，无法从不规范的自然语言需求直接生成结构化安全拓扑，且缺乏相应的公共评测基准。

Method: 提出 TopoIntent 系统：(1) 使用模式契约约束生成；(2) 通过稠密向量检索从精选模板库获取参考架构；(3) 采用分段融合实现意图-模板对齐与安全补全；(4) 依据拓扑层可见的 CIS Controls v8.1.2 防护项进行合规检查，未解决项标记人工复核；(5) 通过加法式模式保持编辑修复结构缺口；(6) 导出为带内核级 iptables ACL 的 Mininet 脚本以支持可执行的可达性与允许/拒绝测试。

Result: 在自建的留出集（7 个模板、14 个金融与政府场景意图）上：加法式修复使拓扑可见 CIS 满足率从 0.78 提升至 1.00，平均不足 1.5 轮；一轮反馈将 ACL 后策略通过率从 0.78 提升至 0.88。检索集含 22 个模板、44 个合成意图，覆盖五个场景。

Conclusion: TopoIntent 证明通过约束生成、模板检索、分段融合与结构修复，能够从欠规范的自然语言需求自动生成合规的安全拓扑，但仍存在需人工复核的未决情形，可执行验证为需求到拓扑任务提供了可靠的评测途径。

Abstract: Enterprise security topology design requires translating business intent, regulatory requirements, and risk assumptions into zones, boundary devices, inter-zone paths, and access-control policies. Existing NetOps automation tools mainly operate after this design is fixed, providing limited support for generating structured security topologies from underspecified natural-language requirements. We present TopoIntent, a system that compiles security intent into executable, compliance-checked network topologies. It uses a schema contract to constrain generation, retrieves reference architectures from a curated template library via dense-vector search, and applies staged fusion for intent-template alignment and security completion. The generated topology is checked against CIS Controls v8.1.2 safeguards visible at the topology layer, while unresolved cases are marked for manual review. Structural gaps are repaired through additive schema-preserving edits. The final topology is exported to Mininet scripts with kernel-level iptables ACLs, enabling executable reachability and allow/deny tests. Because no public benchmark exists for this requirement-to-topology task, we construct an evaluation set from reference security architecture diagrams. The retrieval set contains 22 templates and 44 synthetic intents across five scenarios, while the held-out set contains 7 templates and 14 intents from finance and government scenarios excluded from retrieval. On the held-out set, additive repair improves topology-visible CIS satisfaction from 0.78 to 1.00 in fewer than 1.5 rounds on average, and one feedback round raises the post-ACL policy pass rate from 0.78 to 0.88.

</details>


### [238] [Jointly Predicting Courses and Grades Using a Transformer-Based Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13409)
*Paul Savala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE——一种用于学业成绩预测的Transformer模型，核心创新是联合预测学生下学期的选课集合与各科成绩，从而捕捉同一学期内课程并发的相互影响，相比仅预测成绩的模型将平均绝对误差降低近50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习分析领域的预测模型通常将学生学业历史视为简单的时间序列，忽略了同一学期内多门课程并发的特性。这种简化在课程负担重或难度大的学生身上会导致成绩预测不准确，因此需要一个能建模课程并发效应的模型。

Method: 提出TRACE（TRansformer for Academic Course-grade Estimation），按学期对课程进行编码以捕捉课程并发效应；设计了一种将课程集合预测与成绩预测相结合的新型损失函数；在训练阶段同时预测所修课程及其成绩。模型基于十年机构数据训练，并可自然融入学生属性信息，支持在新机构通过重训练和重校准进行适配。

Result: 与仅预测成绩的相同架构相比，联合预测模型将平均绝对误差降低了近50%；性能优于传统的基于LSTM的序列模型以及基于图神经网络的方法；证明在训练中预测所修课程是提升预测质量的关键技术。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了现代神经架构在构建可解释模型上的价值，模型可通过重训练和重校准迁移至新机构，并有望被整合到高等教育机构的早期预警/检测系统中，用于改进学业成绩预测与学业风险干预。

Abstract: Existing predictive models in learning analytics often treat student academic history as a simple sequence, overlooking the concurrent nature of courses taken within a semester. This simplification can lead to inaccurate performance predictions, particularly for students with heavy or challenging course loads. This paper introduces a TRansformer for Academic Course-grade Estimation (TRACE) that addresses this limitation by jointly predicting both the set of courses a student will take and their corresponding grades for an upcoming semester. Our approach encodes courses on a per-semester basis to capture the effects of course concurrency and utilizes a novel loss function combining course-set prediction with grade prediction. We demonstrate that predicting courses taken in addition to the grades in those courses leads to significant improvements in prediction quality. Trained on ten years of institutional data, our joint prediction model reduces mean absolute error by nearly 50% compared to an identical architecture that predicts grades alone. The model also outperforms traditional LSTM-based sequential models, as well as graph neural network-based approaches, and offers natural ways to incorporate student attribute data. This work demonstrates the utility of modern neural architectures for creating interpretable models that can be adapted to new institutions via retraining and recalibration, as well as the importance of key techniques, such as predicting courses taken during training. We discuss how this model could be incorporated into early detection systems at institutions of higher education.

</details>


### [239] [Who Speaks Matters: Authority-Aware Multi-View RAG over Italian Parliamentary Proceedings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13410)
*Mirko Tritella,Riccardo Pozzi,Matteo Palmonari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ParliamentRAG 是一个面向意大利众议院议会记录的多视角检索增强生成系统，核心是话题相关权威模型，以公正覆盖政治团体、忠实引用并综合专家观点，在多项评测上优于 Google NotebookLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 议会记录是民主审议的一手资料，但体量庞大且高度碎片化，令公民、记者和研究者难以进行多视角获取。将 RAG 直接应用于议会记录会产生三个具体风险：高频发言者占据主导、无法按话题专业性为发言者加权、以及政治敏感文本中的引用归属错误。

Method: 提出 ParliamentRAG 系统，核心是"话题依赖权威模型"——根据当前查询估计每位发言者的权威度，融合职业、教育背景和既往发言记录等可解释成分。系统流程为：检索相关发言片段、跨议会党团识别话题相关专家、生成综合多方视角的摘要，并附支持性引文。

Result: 在 15 个政策议题上，通过自动化指标与六位领域专家的盲测 A/B 人工评测双重协议，与 Google NotebookLM 对比：政治团体覆盖率更高（0.97 vs 0.95）、引文忠实度满分（1.00 vs 0.95）、在来源相关维度获专家更强偏好，而 NotebookLM 在行文相关维度表现更强。

Conclusion: ParliamentRAG 通过话题依赖权威模型有效缓解了 RAG 应用于议会记录时的三大风险，显著提升了政治团体覆盖公平性和引用忠实性，并验证了针对多视角政治文本定制权威建模的必要性。

Abstract: Parliamentary proceedings are a primary record of democratic deliberation, yet their volume and fragmentation make multi-perspective access difficult for citizens, journalists, and researchers. Applying Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to parliamentary transcripts introduces three specific risks: dominance of the most frequent speakers, inability to weight speakers according to topical expertise, and citation misattribution in politically sensitive text. We present ParliamentRAG, a RAG system for the Italian Chamber of Deputies that addresses these risks jointly. Its core contribution is a topic-dependent authority model that estimates each speaker's authority as a function of the current query, combining interpretable components such as profession, education, and previous interventions. Given a user query, the system retrieves relevant speech chunks, identifies topic-relevant experts across parliamentary groups, and generates a summary synthesizing their perspectives, accompanied by supporting quotations. ParliamentRAG is evaluated against Google NotebookLM on 15 policy topics via a two-level protocol combining automated metrics and blind A/B human evaluation by six domain experts. The system achieves higher coverage across political groups (0.97 vs. 0.95), perfect quotation faithfulness (1.00 vs. 0.95), and stronger expert preferences on source-related dimensions, while NotebookLM remains stronger on prose-oriented dimensions.

</details>


### [240] [Beyond Final Scores: A Systematic Evaluation of Agents for Long-Horizon AI Research and Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13417)
*Yiwei Li,Wanli Yang,Hexiang Tan,Xiangzhou Huang,Zhengyu Chen,Ziran Li,Borun Chen,Shanglin Lei,Huaisheng Zhu,Hao Tian,Fei Sun,Xunliang Cai,Jingang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对7个前沿模型在36个长周期任务上进行系统评估，提出新框架用规则化指标刻画运行内行为（方案制定、执行、反馈控制），并评估跨任务经验复用。结果显示当前智能体更像工程优化器而非完全自主的研究者：能制定并实现实用方案，但跨运行表现波动大、最强方案主要改编或组合既有技术、真正的方法论创新罕见。分析揭示相似最终成绩背后存在不同的流程瓶颈、经验复用既可能帮助也可能误导后续决策、以及测试框架设计影响性能稳定性，并据此提出模型训练、推理策略、经验管理和框架设计的改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估仅关注最终分数，既无法揭示进展在何处获得或丧失，也无法判断积累的经验是否改善了后续决策。为理解自主智能体通过长周期实验改进模型、系统等人工产物的能力现状，需要超越最终分数、深入到运行过程和经验复用的系统评估。

Method: 提出一个新的评估框架，针对7个前沿模型在36个长周期任务上进行系统评估。框架采用基于规则的指标，通过三个维度刻画运行内行为：(1)方案制定(Solution Framing)、(2)执行(Execution)、(3)反馈控制(Feedback Control)；同时通过受控对比来评估任务内与跨任务的经验复用。

Result: 当前智能体更像工程优化器而非完全自主的研究者：它们能制定并实现实用方案，但性能在运行间差异显著；最强方案主要来自对既有技术的改编或组合，真正的方法论创新罕见。详细分析表明：相似的最终结果背后存在不同的流程瓶颈；经验复用既能帮助也能误导后续决策；测试框架的设计会影响性能稳定性。

Conclusion: 研究为改进模型训练、推理时策略、经验管理以及测试框架设计提供了具体方向。评估不应止步于最终分数，而应关注运行内行为与经验复用机制，以更准确地刻画自主智能体的长期实验能力。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly capable of improving models, systems, and other technical artifacts through long-horizon experimentation. To understand the current state of this capability, however, evaluation must go beyond final scores, which neither reveal where progress is gained or lost nor indicate whether accumulated experience improves later decisions. We therefore present a systematic evaluation of seven frontier models on 36 long-horizon tasks based on a new framework that uses rule-based metrics to characterize within-run behavior through Solution Framing, Execution, and Feedback Control and controlled comparisons to assess experience reuse within and across tasks. The results show that current agents operate more like engineering optimizers than fully autonomous researchers: they can formulate and implement practical solutions, but their performance varies substantially across runs, their strongest solutions mainly adapt or combine established techniques, and genuine methodological novelty remains rare. Detailed analysis reveals that observed performance is shaped by multiple factors, including distinct process bottlenecks behind similar final outcomes, experience reuse that can help or mislead subsequent decisions, and harness designs that affect performance stability. These findings suggest concrete directions for improving model training, inference-time strategies, experience management, and harness design.

</details>


### [241] [Enhancing Virtual Agents through SLMs and Edge-Computing: An Exploratory Evaluation of Think and Memory Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13420)
*Aimilios Hadjiliasi,Louis Nisiotis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于边缘计算和小语言模型（SLM）的虚拟智能体网关系统，部分实现CEAA框架中的"思考"与"记忆"过程，并在NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX上验证了其在交互式虚拟世界中的路由准确性、记忆读取性能与延迟表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能虚拟代理需要在虚拟世界和元宇宙中具备持久、自适应、情境感知的认知能力，但实现认知完备的代理在概念与技术上都极具挑战。结合边缘计算和生成式AI的最新进展，作者希望利用小语言模型在边缘设备上支撑CEAA核心认知组件，实现低延迟、可本地部署的代理"大脑"。

Method: 基于认知具身代理架构（CEAA），开发一个边缘虚拟代理网关系统，聚焦"Think"（思考）与"Memory"（记忆）两个核心过程。系统部署于NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX边缘设备，采用不同规模的Qwen2.5小语言模型，处理服务请求并驱动记忆型对话；通过一系列仿真实验评估路由准确性、记忆读取性能和系统延迟。

Result: 实验在Jetson Orin NX上验证了SLM驱动的原型代理系统，测试了不同规模Qwen2.5模型下的路由准确性、记忆读取性能与延迟指标，证明该边缘方案能够部分实现CEAA的思考与记忆过程，支持具身代理在沉浸式虚拟世界中进行高效且情境化的认知操作。

Conclusion: 本文展示了利用小语言模型在边缘设备上实现CEAA关键认知组件（思考与记忆）的可行性与有效性，为构建认知"大脑"可在沉浸式虚拟世界中高效、情境化运行的具身代理提供了实现路径，并为未来虚拟与元宇宙环境中的持久化智能代理开发奠定基础。

Abstract: Embodied intelligent virtual agents are expected to operate as persistent, adaptive, and context-aware entities within complex virtual and Metaverse worlds. However, implementing cognitively capable agents in such environments is conceptually and technologically challenging. Among a range of blueprints and development approaches, the Cognitive Embodied Agent Architecture (CEAA) has been developed as an implementation-oriented framework for architecting components of perception, memory, reasoning, planning, and embodied action. Considering the recent advances in edge computing and generative AI language models, this paper explores the use of Small Language Models (SLMs) to support edge-based operation of selected CEAA components, focusing on "Think" and "Memory" as processes central to cognitive orchestration and persistence of virtual agents in interactive virtual worlds. An edge-based virtual agent gateway system was developed and evaluated on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX using Qwen2.5 models of different sizes, exploring the system's capability to process service requests and handle memory-driven conversations. A series of simulation experiments evaluated routing accuracy, memory-read performance, and latency, demonstrating an SLM-driven prototype agent system that partially implements selected CEAA processes to support the development of embodied agents whose cognitive "brain" can operate efficiently and contextually for interactive experiences in immersive virtual worlds.

</details>


### [242] [RAIL: An Automatic Classifier of the Artificial Intelligence Readiness Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13428)
*Juan Irving Vasquez,Juan Terven,Laura-Ivoone Garay-Jimenez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文统一了三种异质的AI成熟度评估框架，提出九级序数尺度"统一AI就绪水平（AIRL）"，并设计"RAIL"多智能体LLM分类器，通过证据代理、六个维度代理与首席专家分级裁决，实现仅凭自然语言描述即可判定AI工作就绪水平，避免单一LLM分类器的高估问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI技术成熟度评估对投资决策、项目管理和政策监测至关重要，但现有就绪框架异质且难以自动应用：TRL对AI的适配缺乏AI专属门槛标准、MLTRL依赖内部过程产物、AI/数据就绪维度模型的量表难以直接比较。

Method: 提出两项贡献：一是将TRL、MLTRL、AI/数据就绪维度模型统一为九级序数尺度AIRL，建立基于环境证据阶梯的框架，辅以维度上限（规格、数据存在性、数据质量、数据合法性、专业知识、算法成熟度）及通用性锚定规则和显式赋值规则，使就绪水平仅凭自然语言描述即可判定；二是提出RAIL多智能体分类器，由1个证据代理和6个独立维度代理（各为窄职责LLM）给出判定，确定性最小规则聚合，首席专家在不对称权威下复核（可确认或降低建议，但不得高于维度上限）。

Result: 方法在多个研究工作的分析中得到测试，表现出良好的一致性，并避免了单体LLM分类器的高估问题。

Conclusion: 统一的AIRL尺度配合RAIL多智能体分类器，能够仅依据自然语言工作描述自动、一致且不夸大（不高于维度上限）地判定AI就绪水平。

Abstract: Assessing the maturity of artificial intelligence technologies is essential for investment decisions, project management, and policy monitoring, yet the available readiness frameworks are heterogeneous and difficult to apply automatically: the adaptation of Technology Readiness Levels to AI lacks AI-specific gating criteria, the Machine Learning Technology Readiness Levels presuppose access to internal process artifacts, and AI/data readiness dimension models employ scales that resist direct comparison. This paper makes two contributions. First, we unify these three frameworks into the Unified AI Readiness Level (AIRL), a nine-level ordinal scale built on an environmental evidence ladder and complemented by dimensional caps (covering specification, data existence, data quality, data legality, expert knowledge, and algorithmic maturity) together with a generality-anchoring rule and explicit assignment disciplines, so that a readiness level becomes decidable from a natural-language description of the work alone. Second, we propose RAIL (Readiness Assessment via Independent LLM-experts), a panel-of-experts classifier that operationalizes the scale: one evidence agent and six independent dimension agents, each a large language model with a narrowly scoped mandate, deliver verdicts that a deterministic minimum rule aggregates and a chief expert reviews under asymmetric authority, confirming or lowering the panel's recommendation but never raising it above the caps. The method was tested in the analysis of several research works showing consistency and avoiding overestimation from monolithic LLM classifiers.

</details>


### [243] [Academic League of Artificial Intelligence - An Integrative Perspective of Teaching, Research, and Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13447)
*Alison R. Panisson,Maria Eduarda W. M. Vianna,Italo Firmino da Silva,Heitor Henrique da Silva,Rafaela Fernandes Savaris,Bernardo Pandolfi Costa,Martin Augusto Gagliotti Vigil,Jim Lau,Agenor Hentz,Andréa Sabedra Bordin,Alexandre Leopoldo Gonçalves,Roberto Rodrigues-Filho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了巴西圣卡塔琳娜联邦大学（UFSC）人工智能学术联盟（LIA）的组织框架，该框架以学生为中心、基于项目，将教学、科研和大学推广三大支柱整合为一体，并提供了可复制推广的实践模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术联盟已成为促进课外教育、加强大学与社会融合的重要机制。然而，如何在统一的组织框架下有效整合教学、科研与大学推广三大支柱，同时培养学生的技术能力与横向能力（如领导力、协作与社区参与），仍缺乏系统化的实践指导。

Method: 提出一个以学生为中心、基于项目的组织框架，融合民主治理、协作学习和动态项目管理。框架通过竞赛团队、学习小组、公开讲座、知识库以及具有社会影响力的人工智能应用等代表性举措进行验证，覆盖了教育、科研和推广三类活动。

Result: 实践表明该框架能够在共同的组织结构下支撑多样化的教育、科研与推广活动，同时有效促进领导力培养、科学生产、社区参与和知识留存。所报告的实践经验显示该框架具有灵活性和可复制性，适用于工程与计算机教育领域。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为将大学三大支柱（教学、科研、推广）整合进工程与计算机教育提供了一种灵活且可复制的模式，并为学术联盟及类似学生组织提供了切实可行的实践指导。

Abstract: Academic leagues have become important mechanisms for promoting extracurricular education and strengthening the integration between universities and society. This paper presents the organizational framework adopted by the Academic League of Artificial Intelligence (LIA) at the Federal University of Santa Catarina (UFSC), designed to integrate teaching, research, and university extension through a student-centered, project-based approach. The framework combines democratic governance, collaborative learning, and dynamic project organization to foster both technical and transversal competencies. The framework is illustrated through representative initiatives, including competition teams, study groups, open lectures, knowledge repositories, and AI-powered applications with social impact. These projects demonstrate how diverse educational, scientific, and extension activities can be developed within a common organizational structure while promoting leadership, scientific production, community engagement, and knowledge preservation. The reported experience indicates that the proposed framework provides a flexible and replicable model for integrating the three university pillars into engineering and computing education, offering practical guidance for academic leagues and similar student organizations.

</details>


### [244] [A Unifying Perspective on Causal World Models: From Observations to Representations to Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13456)
*Avinash Kori,Fabrizio Russo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从因果视角对世界模型（World Models, WM）进行多层抽象研究，提出因果世界模型（CWM）的正式定义，并阐明其在数据中的可识别性，以支持因果推理与知情决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前世界模型往往只强调生成能力，缺乏对系统动力学背后因果结构的刻画。作者认为有用的世界模型必须超越纯粹生成，还须捕获实体属性、实体间交互及实体与环境的交互，从而真正解释并决定系统动力学。

Method: 从感知观测到环境动力学结构的概念表征，对多个抽象层次的世界模型进行因果分析；将世界建模与因果表征学习、以对象为中心的学习、因果发现、结构因果模型及基于模型的决策等既有工作相连接；给出依托其目标任务而定基的因果世界模型（CWM）正式定义。

Result: 给出了 CWM 的正式定义，并建立了其与可识别性文献的关联，明确了世界模型各组成部分在何种条件下、以何种等价程度可从数据中恢复。

Conclusion: 通过将世界模型建立于支持因果推理和知情决策的表征与结构之上，CWM 为超越训练分布进行预测、规划与行动提供了更坚实的概念基础。

Abstract: World Models (WM) are increasingly seen as a foundation for intelligent agents that can predict, plan, and act beyond their training distribution. In this paper, we study WMs from a causal perspective across multiple levels of abstraction, ranging from perceptual observations to building a conceptual representation of the structure governing the environment dynamics. We argue that useful WMs must go beyond generative capabilities alone: they should also capture entity properties, entity-to-entity interactions, and entity-to-environment interactions that determine and explain the dynamics of a system. We provide a formal definition of Causal WMs (CWMs) grounded in the tasks they are intended to support, connecting world modelling with existing work in causal representation learning, object-centric learning, causal discovery, structural causal models, and model-based decision-making. Finally, we relate CWMs to the literature on identifiability, clarifying when the components of a WM can be recovered from data and up to which equivalence. With this, we ground WMs in representations and structures that support causal reasoning and informed decision-making.

</details>


### [245] [MARC v1: An Open-Source Multi-Agent Framework for Clinical AI Reasoning and Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13476)
*Saisha Shetty,Satvik Tripathi,Austin Lin,Colin Zhao,Theodore Kim,Don Enwerem,Jacinta Arnold,Shahriar Faghani,Tessa S Cook*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MARC 是一个开源的多智能体临床推理框架，用确定性的多智能体编排取代单一 LLM 提示，实现角色分工、可追溯的中间输出与阶段级故障归因，无需编程即可配置和部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 临床推理通常依赖单体化的提示工程，缺乏透明性、可解释性与故障定位能力；同时临床领域专家往往不具备编程或提示工程技能。作者旨在用一个可解释、模型无关、无需代码即可使用的多智能体框架来替代整体式 LLM 调用。

Method: 提出 MARC 框架，协调面向提取、推理、答案生成和评估等角色的专用智能体，在智能体之间显式传递上下文并输出可追溯的中间结果；引入 Decomposer 模块，从自然语言描述自动生成任务专属的智能体提示，消除手工提示工程。框架同时支持基于 API 的部署和本地 CPU 兼容部署，全部通过 YAML 配置完成，无需修改代码。

Result: 实现了一个可用的开源框架，能够进行分阶段的失败归因，支持 API 与本地 CPU 两种部署方式，且对临床领域专家零编程门槛；完整框架已在 GitHub 开源。

Conclusion: MARC 通过确定性多智能体编排、可追溯性与自动提示生成，实现了模型无关、可解释且对非编程用户友好的临床推理框架，代码已开源供复现和使用。

Abstract: We present Multi-Agent Reasoning and Coordination (MARC), an open-source framework that replaces monolithic LLM prompting with deterministic multi-agent orchestration for clinical reasoning. MARC coordinates role-specialized agents for extraction, reasoning, answer generation, and evaluation, with explicit context passing and traceable intermediate outputs, enabling stage-wise failure attribution. We additionally introduce a Decomposer module that generates task-specific agent prompts from a plain-language description, eliminating manual prompt engineering. The framework supports both API-based and local CPU-compatible deployments and is entirely configurable via YAML, without code modifications. MARC is designed to be model-agnostic, interpretable, and accessible to clinical domain experts without programming expertise. The full framework is available at https://github.com/Penn-RAIL/MARC-v1.

</details>


### [246] [AlayaWorld: Interactive Long-Horizon World Modeling - Full Technical Report (v1.1)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13492)
*AlayaWorld Team,Kaipeng Zhang,Chuanhao Li,Yifan Zhan,Yongtao Ge,Yuanyang Yin,Jiaming Tan,Kang He,Liaoyuan Fan,Mingliang Zhai,Ruicong Liu,Xiaojie Xu,Xuangeng Chu,Zhen Li,Zhengyuan Lin,Zhixiang Wang,Zian Meng,Zihui Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AlayaWorld的改进版本，在保持骨干架构、分块自回归生成方案和训练数据不变的前提下，重点重构了条件信号的表示与融合方式，核心原则是让条件信号与生成内容在潜在表示和时间结构上尽可能一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上一版AlayaWorld中条件信号（如空间记忆、图像条件、相机控制等）与生成视频在潜在表示和时间结构上存在不匹配，制约了生成质量与一致性。作者希望以"条件信号应尽可能接近生成内容"为原则，从表示空间和时间结构两个维度统一条件与生成，从而提升视频生成的连贯性和可控性。

Method: 在保持骨干架构、分块自回归生成与训练数据不变的基础上，进行两项重大改动：①用流式3D点缓存渲染器取代基于深度扭曲的空间记忆；②重构条件流水线，使视觉条件在与因果VAE相同的潜在空间中编码，且时间统计特性与生成视频一致。具体包含六项修改：(1) 将静态帧图像条件替换为运动感知的潜在条件；(2) 将重新渲染的空间记忆以连续序列进行因果编码；(3) 在像素空间对齐时间记忆窗口；(4) 采用硬记忆丢弃，直接移除记忆token而非置零；(5) 统一训练与推理阶段的VAE编解码协议；(6) 移除相机AdaLN分支，视角控制完全由重新渲染的空间条件提供。

Result: 报告未在摘要中给出具体量化评测结果，但说明了新版通过上述六项修改，使条件信号在潜在表示和时间统计上与生成视频保持一致，并实现了空间记忆的流式3D点缓存渲染以及纯条件驱动的相机视角控制。

Conclusion: 改进版AlayaWorld通过重新设计条件信号的表示与融合方式，遵循"条件与生成内容在潜在表示和时间结构上尽量一致"的原则，系统性地统一了视觉条件、空间记忆与生成视频的编码空间和时间结构，从而改进了世界模型的视频生成能力。

Abstract: This report presents an improved version of AlayaWorld. While the backbone architecture, chunk-wise autoregressive generation scheme, and training data remain unchanged from the previous release, we substantially revise how conditioning signals are represented and integrated into the model. The new design is guided by a simple principle: conditioning signals should match the generated content as closely as possible in both latent representation and temporal structure. To this end, we make two major changes. First, we replace the previous depth-warping-based spatial memory with a streaming 3D point-cache renderer. Second, we redesign the conditioning pipeline so that visual conditions are encoded in the same causal-VAE latent space, with temporal statistics consistent with those of the generated video. Concretely, the new version introduces six modifications: (1) replacing static-frame image conditioning with motion-aware latent conditioning; (2) causally encoding re-rendered spatial memory as a continuous sequence; (3) aligning the temporal-memory window in pixel space; (4) adopting hard memory dropout that removes memory tokens rather than zeroing them; (5) unifying the VAE encoding and decoding protocol across training and inference; and (6) removing the camera AdaLN branch, such that viewpoint control is provided entirely through the re-rendered spatial condition.

</details>


### [247] [QuoteBench: How Matched Scores Can Hide Command-Path Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13547)
*Shangao Li,Yao Zhang,Volker Tresp,Yuanyuan Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: QuoteBench 通过 56 个单次任务（来自 14 个事故衍生家族）精确验证最终状态，量化 LLM 编码代理在命令生成与执行传输之间的边界。结果显示：同样的回复经新增解析器回放后成功率下降 55.4–73.2 个百分点，披露边界后仅 6 个配置恢复 30.4–60.7 点；GPT-5.6-sol 的匹配差距为 -3.6 点，却隐藏了 -64.3 点损伤与 +60.7 点补偿。模型排名因此重新排序。作者主张代理评测应报告模型配置、生成契约、执行路径、工作点与最终状态验证器，而非把匹配得分当作模型的固有属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 编码代理通过可能序列化、包装并重新解析模型输出的接口来发布 Bash 命令。仅靠"匹配执行分数"无法区分命令生成错误与生成之后引入的失败，掩盖了生成契约与执行传输之间的边界问题。

Method: 构建 QuoteBench 基准：56 个单次（one-shot）任务，来自 14 个事故衍生命令家族，用精确的最终状态验证来衡量生成契约与执行传输边界。在单个"故意未转义的附加解析器"周围交叉生成契约与执行传输，在插值点进行转义以复现每次回放回复的原始路径结果；并通过回放同一回复对比"无解析器"与"添加解析器"两种传输路径的差异，测试披露边界后模型是否通过改变生成来自我恢复。在八个同窗口配置上实验。

Result: 回放同一回复经新增解析器后，成功率下降 55.4–73.2 个百分点；披露边界使 6 个配置恢复 30.4–60.7 个百分点，另两个配置为 0 或略负。原始生成已接近前沿饱和，边界适应才是区分模型的关键。GPT-5.6-sol 匹配差距 -3.6 点，实际隐藏 -64.3 点损伤与 +60.7 点补偿。部署配置改变模型排名：26 个可比对中 1 个明确反转、4 个处于单任务边缘。

Conclusion: 命令代理的评测不应把匹配得分当作模型固有属性，应明确报告模型配置、生成契约、执行路径、工作点及最终状态验证器，才能准确区分模型能力与部署/传输边界的影响。

Abstract: LLM coding agents issue Bash commands through interfaces that may serialize, wrap, and reparse model output. Matched execution scores alone cannot distinguish command-generation errors from failures introduced after generation. QuoteBench measures this boundary with exact final-state validation on 56 one-shot tasks from 14 incident-derived families, crossing the generation contract with the execution transport around one deliberately unescaped added parser. Escaping at the interpolation point reproduces each replayed reply's raw-path outcome, so any recovery under a disclosed boundary must come from the model changing its generation. Across eight same-window configurations, replaying the same reply through the added parser lowers success by 55.4 to 73.2 percentage points; disclosure recovers 30.4 to 60.7 points for six configurations, and zero or slightly negative for the other two. Raw generation is nearly saturated at the frontier; boundary adaptation is what still separates models. GPT-5.6-sol's matched gap of -3.6 points hides -64.3 points of damage and +60.7 points of compensation. The deployment configuration reorders models: one reversal among 26 comparable pairs is unambiguous and four more sit on single-task margins. Evaluations of command-issuing agents should report the model configuration, generation contract, execution path, operating point, and final-state validator rather than treat a matched score as an intrinsic model property.

</details>


### [248] [OmniScientist: An Omni-Modal Omni-Discipline AI Scientist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13558)
*Bobo Li,Hao Fei,Tianjie Ju,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OmniScientist 是一个端到端、全模态（omni-modal）的 AI 科学家系统，能够直接从异构原始证据出发完成多学科研究全流程，并通过全生命周期感知显著提升证据驱动的科学发现质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型驱动的 AI 科学家虽能自动化越来越完整的研究工作流，但通常只基于文本、代码、标签或预计算摘要进行推理，导致对科学发现至关重要的空间、时间、跨通道和过程性关系对智能体不可见。作者认为仅覆盖工作流不足以获得科学发现所依赖的完整证据，因此需要让智能体直接感知原始证据。

Method: 提出 OmniScientist，采用确定性流水线架构，包含三层：感知层（perception layer）和 3 个自主智能体（构思 ideation、实验 experiment、撰写 writeup）。原始观察贯穿研究全生命周期，影响研究问题、实验决策和最终结论。系统在代码中运行 idea、rigour 与 claim 三类检查，强制执行新颖性筛选、统计有效性验证、执行溯源和数值可追溯性。

Result: 在 36 个真实数据案例上评估，覆盖 5 个学科族、4 类科学证据及图像、信号、音频、视频、3D 结构、轨迹、表格、公式和图等多种模态。系统在所有 36 个案例中完成从原始数据到成稿论文的完整流程，使用参考推理主干时平均总论文得分达 6.3。在与仅接收预计算标量特征的盲测变体的配对比较中，直接感知在全部 7 个评估维度上均有提升，并在 85% 的正面交锋判断中胜出。

Conclusion: 全生命周期感知对基于证据的科学发现至关重要，提供了一个通向功能更全面的 AI 科学家的实用路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in foundation models have enabled AI scientists to automate increasingly complete research workflows, from hypothesis generation and code execution to manuscript preparation. Yet workflow coverage alone does not provide access to the full evidence on which scientific discovery depends. Existing systems typically reason over text, code, labels, or precomputed summaries, leaving scientifically decisive spatial, temporal, cross-channel, and procedural relations unavailable to the agent. We introduce OmniScientist, an end-to-end, omni-modal AI scientist that conducts multidisciplinary research directly from heterogeneous raw evidence. A perception layer and 3 autonomous agents for ideation, experiment, and writeup operate within a deterministic pipeline, allowing observations to shape research questions, experimental decisions, and final claims throughout the research lifecycle. By running idea, rigour, and claim checks in code, the system enforces novelty screening, statistical validity, execution provenance, and numerical traceability. We evaluate OmniScientist on 36 real-data cases spanning 5 discipline families, 4 families of scientific evidence, and modalities including images, signals, audio, video, 3-D structures, trajectories, tables, formulae, and graphs. The system completes the full path from raw data to a compiled manuscript in all 36 cases and achieves a mean overall paper score of 6.3 with the reference reasoning backbone. In paired comparisons against a blind variant that receives only precomputed scalar features, direct perception improves all 7 evaluation dimensions and wins 85% of head-to-head judgments. These results show that lifecycle-wide perception is essential for evidence-grounded scientific discovery and provides a practical path toward broadly capable AI scientists.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [249] [Adaptable Fingerprinting with Nonlinear Shrinkage for Climate Change Detection and Attribution under Variance Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12496)
*Haoran Li,Yan Li*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对气候变化检测与归因中的指纹法（线性变量含误差回归）问题，本文提出一种非线性旋转不变的精度矩阵收缩估计方法，在高维情形下恢复总体最小二乘估计量的渐近最优性，同时联合估计尺度因子与变率膨胀因子，从而纠正估计偏差并实现有效的置信区间与模型充分性检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在高维情形下常忽视预测变量的方差膨胀，且精度矩阵估计不精确，导致尺度因子（回归系数）估计有偏、不确定性被低估、置信区间覆盖不足，影响气候变化检测与归因的可靠性。

Method: 提出非线性、旋转不变的收缩框架来估计回归误差的精度矩阵，以恢复总体最小二乘估计量在高维条件下的渐近最优性；联合估计尺度因子与变率膨胀因子以纠正偏差；引入一致方差估计量用于有效的不确定性量化；并开发残差一致性检验以评估模型充分性。

Result: 数值研究表明该方法实现精确估计、改善置信区间覆盖并提高效率。应用于1951–2020年年平均近地表气温数据时，方法得到更窄且更可靠的置信区间，从而产生更精细的归因结果。

Conclusion: 所提收缩精度矩阵估计方法能有效纠正高维指纹法中的估计偏差、改善不确定性量化与置信区间覆盖，并在实际气温数据上给出更可靠的归因结论，优于现有方法。

Abstract: Detection and attribution of climate change relies on fingerprinting--a linear errors-in-variables regression framework in which both predictors and responses exhibit internal variability governed by a proportional covariance structure, subject to a variability inflation factor. Accurate estimation of the scaling factors (regression coefficients) depends on inferring the precision matrix of the regression errors from limited climate model control runs. In high-dimensional settings, existing approaches often overlook the variance inflation of the predictors and suffer from imprecise precision matrix estimates, yielding biased estimators, underestimated uncertainties, and confidence intervals with poor coverage. We propose a nonlinear, rotation-invariant shrinkage framework for estimating the precision matrix that restores the asymptotic optimality of the total least squares estimator in high-dimensional regimes. Our procedure jointly estimates the scaling factors and the variability inflation factor, thereby correcting estimation bias, and incorporates consistent variance estimators to enable valid uncertainty quantification. We also develop a residual consistency test to assess model adequacy. Numerical studies demonstrate precise estimation, improved confidence interval coverage, and higher efficiency. Applied to annual mean near-surface air temperature data from 1951--2020, our method produces narrower and more reliable confidence intervals, yielding refined attribution results.

</details>


### [250] [Bayesian Modeling of Gibbs Point Processes via Basis Function Expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12510)
*Christopher Hassett,Athanasios C. Micheas,Scott H. Holan,Stamatis Dostoglou*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层贝叶斯框架，用于非齐次成对交互的 Gibbs 点过程模型，并通过基函数展开对全局与局部效应函数建模，同时给出检验完全空间随机性的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点过程建模中常需同时刻画空间非齐次强度（一阶效应）与点间交互（二阶效应），而传统参数模型对全局和局部效应函数的设定过于刚性，难以灵活刻画真实数据中的复杂空间结构，因此需要一个更具灵活性的分层贝叶斯框架。

Method: 构建分层贝叶斯非齐次成对交互 Gibbs 点过程模型，利用基函数展开对全局效应函数和局部效应函数进行非参数化建模；并在此基础上提出检验程序，用于判断数据是否服从完全空间随机（CSR）假设。

Result: 该方法通过两个真实基准数据集（水黾分布数据与森林火灾数据）进行实证说明，展示了模型拟合与完全空间随机性检验的实际效果。

Conclusion: 所提出的分层贝叶斯框架能够灵活地对非齐次成对交互点过程建模，并提供了有效的完全空间随机性检验工具，适用于真实空间点模式数据分析。

Abstract: We present a hierarchical Bayesian framework for non-homogeneous pairwise interaction Gibbs point process models, where the global and local effect functions are modeled via basis function expansions. We further propose a testing procedure in order to assess complete spatial randomness. The proposed methodology is exemplified through two real benchmark data examples involving water striders and forest fires.

</details>


### [251] [Empirical Simulation of Survival and Mixed-Type Data for Clinical Trial Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12641)
*Yao Chen,Jiren Sun,Yuxin Ding,Yushi Liu,Yongming Qu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于经验 copula 的框架，用于模拟包含连续、二元、计数及右删失生存变量的多元临床数据，并通过两阶段填补与目标分布匹配来重建试验组的生存曲线，已在 R 包 EmpiricalSim 中实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法从 Weibull 或对数正态等参数族抽样生成事件时间，导致危险率形状受限、难以真实反映观测生存数据，无法满足复杂临床试验设计与评估对仿真数据的需求。

Method: 作者采用基于经验 copula 的框架：先用两区段（two-zone）程序补全删失历史生存数据，结合条件 Kaplan-Meier 填补与参数化尾部；再通过对经验分位数函数的对数尺度位置-尺度变换和幂扭曲匹配目标生存分布；并以拟合秩相关的高斯 copula 保留历史数据间的依赖结构。

Result: 在一项既往治疗的非小细胞肺癌肿瘤试验中，该方法仅利用对照组数据与少量目标百分位数即可重建试验组的总生存和无进展生存曲线；仿真保持基线协变量的秩相关以及 PFS 与 OS 之间的依赖性，删失 Kendall's tau 为 0.522，与观测数据的 0.549 接近。

Conclusion: 该方法能够生成既贴合目标生存分布又保留变量间依赖结构的逼真多元生存数据，为复杂临床试验方案的规划与评估提供有效工具，并已实现为 R 包 EmpiricalSim。

Abstract: Simulating realistic time-to-event data is essential for planning and evaluating complex clinical trial designs. Conventional approaches often sample event times from parametric families, such as Weibull or log-normal distributions, which restrict hazard shapes and may poorly represent observed survival data. We propose an empirical copula-based framework for simulating multivariate data containing continuous, binary, count, and right-censored time-to-event variables. The method completes censored historical survival data using a two-zone procedure that combines conditional Kaplan-Meier imputation with a parametric tail. It matches a target survival distribution through a log-scale location-scale transformation and a power distortion of the empirical percentile function, while preserving historical dependence through a Gaussian copula fitted to rank correlations. In an oncology trial of previously treated non-small-cell lung cancer, the method reconstructs overall survival and progression-free survival curves for the experimental arm using control-arm data and a small set of target percentiles. Simulations preserve rank correlations among baseline covariates and the dependence between progression-free and overall survival, with censored Kendall's tau of 0.522 compared with 0.549 in the observed data. The method is implemented in the R package EmpiricalSim.

</details>


### [252] [Distinguishing case-mix from context heterogeneity in prognostic regression model synthesis settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12885)
*Max Behrens,Janis M. Nolde,Eleni Papakonstantinou,Gabriele Bellerino,Theodoros Evrenoglou,Angelika Rohde,Daiana Stolz,Moritz Hess,Harald Binder*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种诊断预后回归模型中系数异质性来源的方法，区分病例组合（case-mix）异质性与情境（contextual）异质性，并在 COPD 两站点试验上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多站点/联邦/个体数据荟萃分析中，分析师常需判断应使用单一回归模型还是站点特化模型。现有指标（如系数级 tau²）虽能度量异质性大小，却无法区分异质性究竟源于病例组合还是站点特异的背景作用，导致建模决策缺乏依据。

Method: 在降维空间中拟合站点特异的局部回归，将平滑后的系数曲面分解为跨站点参考部分与站点特异偏差；利用自编码器和自定义损失函数，使潜空间围绕局部预后关系结构化；再将分解结果投影到结局尺度，导出观测级与站点级汇总。另通过置换站点标签的阴性对照评估情境汇总是否可能由无信号的站点标签产生。

Result: 在包含两个站点的 COPD 试验数据上，三个主要潜斜率坐标中的系数曲面变异主要以情境性为主；观测级结局尺度的方差分解以病例组合为主导，而站点间聚合则集中于情境差异而非病例组合偏移。

Conclusion: 该诊断区分方法有助于判断应评估联合模型还是站点特异回归模型，为多站点预后建模中的异质性溯源提供了新工具。

Abstract: Prognostic regression models often synthesize data from multiple sites, whether within a multi-site study, across federated settings, or in individual participant data meta-analysis. Here, a site is any data source, such as a hospital, registry, trial, or study, and need not be a physical center. Analysts must then decide whether one regression model represents all sites or whether site-specific models are needed. Established measures such as coefficient-level tau^2 quantify heterogeneity but do not distinguish its source. We focus on diagnosing whether coefficient heterogeneity reflects case-mix or site-specific context effects. Case-mix heterogeneity can arise when linear regression terms approximate multivariable non-linear relationships in populations with different covariate distributions. Contextual heterogeneity arises when comparable patients require different regression relationships across sites. We do this by fitting site-specific local regressions in a dimension-reduced space and partitioning the smoothed coefficient surfaces into a cross-site reference and site-specific deviations. An autoencoder and custom loss structure the latent space around local prognostic relationships. We then project this partition onto the outcome scale to derive observation- and site-level summaries. We demonstrate the approach on a COPD trial with two sites. In the three leading latent slope coordinates, coefficient-surface variation was predominantly contextual. The derived observation-level outcome-scale variance partition was case-mix-leading, whereas its between-site aggregation was concentrated in contextual differences rather than case-mix shifts. A permuted-site negative control assesses whether the contextual summary can arise when site labels carry no signal. This diagnostic distinction can inform whether joint or site-specific regression models should be evaluated.

</details>


### [253] [A Revisit to Point Estimation Through the Empirical Bayes Method: The Case of Binomial Distribution with Beta Prior and Extension to Poisson Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12922)
*Quoc-Bao Nguyen,Nabendu Pal,Dang Van Vinh*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文重新审视经验贝叶斯方法在二项分布参数估计中的应用，证明在二次损失函数下使用两参数Beta先验族时，Type-II极大似然（ML-II）步骤失效；即使限制为一参数对称Beta先验族，所得经验贝叶斯估计量相对MLE也无显著优势，并将该研究扩展至Poisson模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经验贝叶斯方法被视为经典频率派与完全贝叶斯方法之间的折衷，被广泛建议和采用。但其核心问题——经验贝叶斯方法是否真的能产生改进的估计量（即经验贝叶斯估计量EBE）——尚未得到严格回答。作者旨在通过广泛引用的二项参数估计简单问题来检验这一根本性问题。

Method: 在二次损失函数下，采用常规的两参数Beta先验族估计二项分布参数，先证明Type-II极大似然（ML-II）步骤不成立；进一步将先验限制为一参数对称Beta族，并通过大量计算将所得经验贝叶斯估计量与最大似然估计（MLE）进行比较；最后将该二项研究框架扩展到Poisson模型中进行类似分析。

Result: 1）在二项参数估计中，两参数Beta先验下的ML-II步骤失效；2）即便采用一参数对称Beta先验族，所得经验贝叶斯估计量相对MLE并未表现出显著改进的性能，详细计算结果支持这一结论；3）Poisson模型的扩展研究得到了与二项情形一致的结论。

Conclusion: 经验贝叶斯方法在本文所研究的场景（Beta/Poisson先验、二次损失）下并不能保证产生优于MLE的估计量，ML-II步骤甚至失效，对经验贝叶斯方法作为通用改进手段的有效性提出质疑。

Abstract: Between the classical (frequentist) approach, which is based solely on the data, and a fully Bayesian set-up where one assumes a prior distribution for the model parameters, lies the Empirical Bayes (EB) approach which appears to be a good compromise between the aforementioned two approaches. Even though many researchers have suggested various variants of the EB method, the standard practice is to derive the Bayes estimator under a family of suitable priors indexed by its own parameter(s), called the hyperparameter(s), and then replace the unknown hyperparameter(s) by their estimate(s) obtained from the marginal distribution of the data. But the fundamental question that is being raised here is: does the EB method really work to produce an improved estimator - the so-called Empirical Bayes Estimator (EBE)?
  In this work we are going to revisit the widely cited simple problem of estimating a Binomial parameter using the regular two-parameter Beta family of priors under the quadratic loss function, and prove that the Type-II maximum likelihood (ML-II) step does not work. If we further restrict our attention to one-parameter symmetric Beta family of priors then still the resultant EBE does not show any remarkable performance compared to the MLE details of which have been provided with extensive computations. The Binomial study has been extended to the Poisson model as well.

</details>


### [254] [Bayesian Inference Procedures for A/B Testing: An Overview](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12949)
*Mårten Schultzberg,Mattias Frånberg*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了A/B测试中贝叶斯推断的各类先验与停止规则配置，将其划分为三层等级体系，并通过理论分析与仿真说明各层级的统计性质、代价函数最优性及校准条件，指出误差率、估计精度与后悔值是不同的风险，方法选择应取决于实验项目需要控制的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: A/B测试的贝叶斯推断实际上是一族具有根本不同统计性质的先验与停止规则配置，但业界常将其当作单一方法讨论，且缺乏系统性概览，需要厘清不同配置的层次关系与选择依据。

Method: 将常见配置组织为三层等级体系：（1）后验一致性但无误差控制；（2）通过贝叶斯因子停止在连续监测下控制假阳性率；（3）通过经验贝叶斯实现假发现率控制与校准收缩。作者证明贝叶斯因子停止对包括A/B测试文献中多数提议在内的广泛代价函数类近似最优；并通过仿真将各方法与群序贯、任意时刻有效的频率学派基准对比，检验扁平先验后验停止、校准经验贝叶斯先验及期望损失停止的行为。

Result: 贝叶斯因子停止同时控制假阳性率，因而决策论与频率学派表述之间的选择主要是参数化问题；经验贝叶斯先验是进入第三层的唯一路径，但赢者挑选语料、合并项目与异质指标即使语料规模很大也会破坏校准；仿真显示扁平先验后验停止恰好重现了朴素的反复窥视（peeking），良好校准的经验贝叶斯先验实现最低估计误差，期望损失停止仅在发布零效应变体几乎无成本时最小化后悔值。

Conclusion: 误差率、估计精度与后悔值属于不同的风险，恰当的方法取决于实验项目需要控制的风险，而非本末倒置地由方法决定风险。

Abstract: Bayesian inference for A/B testing is a family of prior and stopping-rule configurations with fundamentally different statistical properties, but it is often discussed as a single method, and no systematic overview exists. This paper organizes common configurations into a three-tier hierarchy: 1) posterior coherence with no error control, 2) false positive rates bounded under continuous monitoring via Bayes factor stopping, and 3) false discovery rate control and calibrated shrinkage via empirical Bayes. Many commercial platforms operate at the lowest tier by default. We show that Bayes factor stopping is near-optimal for a broad class of cost functions, including most proposed in the A/B testing literature; because the same rule also controls the false positive rate, the choice between a decision-theoretic and a frequentist formulation is largely one of parameterization. Furthermore, the empirical Bayes prior is the only path to the third tier, but winner-selected corpora, pooled programs, and heterogeneous metrics can each prevent calibration regardless of corpus size. Simulations against group-sequential and always-valid frequentist baselines show that flat-prior posterior stopping exactly reproduces naive peeking, that a well-calibrated empirical Bayes prior achieves the lowest estimation error, and that expected-loss stopping minimizes regret only when shipping a null-effect variant is nearly free. Error rates, estimation accuracy, and regret are all different risks, and the appropriate method follows from the risks an experimentation program needs to control, not the other way around.

</details>


### [255] [WIRED: Weighted Adaptive Prediction with Structured Dependence for Probabilistic Multiseries Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12998)
*Giancarlo Vercellino*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文介绍 WIRED，一个用于多相关时间序列联合概率预测的 R 包算法：它用简单边际预测分布库、基于 CRPS 的自适应混合权重和高斯或 Student-t copula 实现跨序列模拟。基准实验表明其依赖显式建模架构可行，但当前基于 CRPS 外推的 softmax 加权尚不够稳健，未优于简单 bootstrap 或等权方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多相关时间序列的联合概率预测具有实际价值，但现有方法难以将灵活的自适应边际预测聚合与跨序列的依赖结构重建有效解耦，限制了概率集成方法的稳健性与可扩展性。作者旨在提供一个清晰分离这两层的架构与诊断框架。

Method: WIRED 将框架拆分为三层：① 边际预测库——由一组简单的边际预测分布构成专家（experts）；② 边际聚合层——采用基于 CRPS 的软最大化（softmax）权重外推来自适应混合边际专家；③ 依赖重建层——用高斯或 Student t copula 对聚合后的边际分布进行跨序列联合模拟。实验覆盖 4 个合成数据生成过程（DGP）、3 个预测 horizon、每个 DGP–horizon 组合 30 次重复、9 个消融与外部基线，并在内置 EuStockMarkets 数据上做滚动起点研究。

Result: 基准与诊断显示：分离自适应边际专家聚合与依赖重建的架构是有效的，显式依赖建模得到数据支持；但当前基于 CRPS 外推的 softmax 加权尚不稳健，无法稳定优于更简单的 bootstrap 重采样或等权方案，说明边际聚合层是整体性能瓶颈。

Conclusion: 论文定位出一个可工作的设计层（依赖重建层）与一个瓶颈层（边际聚合层），并提出一条具体研究路径：构建更正则化的概率集成构造方法。当前版本定位为架构与诊断性贡献，而非证明 WIRED 全面优于现有方法。

Abstract: This paper presents WIRED, an R package algorithm for joint probabilistic forecasting of multiple related time series. WIRED combines a library of simple marginal predictive distributions, CRPS-based adaptive mixture weights, and a Gaussian or Student t copula for cross-series simulation. We evaluate the implementation in a benchmark with four synthetic data-generating processes (DGPs), three forecast horizons, 30 replicates per DGP-horizon pair, nine ablations and external baselines, and a rolling-origin study on the built-in EuStockMarkets data. The central contribution is architectural and diagnostic. WIRED separates adaptive marginal expert aggregation from dependence reconstruction; the benchmark supports explicit dependence modeling, but shows that the current CRPS-extrapolated softmax weighting is not yet robust enough to dominate simpler bootstrap or equal-weight alternatives. The paper therefore identifies a working layer of the design, a bottleneck in the marginal aggregation layer, and a concrete research path for more regularized probabilistic ensemble construction.

</details>


### [256] [Causal Mediation Analysis with a Time-Dependent Mediator, Time-Dependent Confounders and a Time-to-Event Outcome: Revisiting the Difference Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13094)
*Robin Denz,Nina Timmesfeld*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文通过模拟研究与真实数据分析，系统评估了"差异法（difference method）"在含时依中介变量和时依混杂因素的时间-事件结局场景中的表现，并以参数化中介 g-公式作为基准比较。结果显示，差异法仅在强假设（如治疗不直接导致时依混杂）且模型正确指定时近似无偏，适用范围有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中介分析可将总效应分解为直接与间接效应，但在时间-事件结局、时依中介与时依混杂场景下，现有方法统计复杂、计算量大且缺乏易用软件。差异法虽简单，但其在该场景下的性能此前未被系统评估，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用模拟研究与真实数据分析，比较基于 Cox 比例风险模型、Aalen 可加风险模型和加速失效时间（AFT）模型（均含时变协变量）的差异法，在不同数据生成过程（含时依中介与时依混杂）下的间接效应估计偏差，并以参数化中介 g-公式作为基准参照。

Result: 在无"由治疗直接引起的时依混杂"且模型正确指定时，基于 Aalen 模型的差异法产生无偏估计；Cox 模型差异法在结局罕见时结果类似，但在常见结局下表现不佳；AFT 模型差异法因可折叠性（collapsibility）问题几乎在所有场景下均有偏。仅参数化中介 g-公式在所有场景下均无偏。

Conclusion: 与专门方法相比，差异法需要额外且往往不切实际的假设（如治疗对时依混杂无直接因果效应）。若这些假设成立，差异法可作为简单高效的替代手段；否则应优先采用参数化中介 g-公式等专门方法。

Abstract: Mediation analysis is a powerful tool to decompose treatment effects into direct and indirect components, enabling explanations of total treatment effects in a formal statistical framework. However, applying such analyses to settings with time-to-event outcomes, time-dependent mediators and confounders remains challenging. Existing methods are statistically complex, computationally intensive, and rarely available in user-friendly software. The difference method offers a simple alternative, but its performance in this setting has not been systematically evaluated. We conducted a simulation study and real-world data analysis to fill this gap in the literature. Using Cox proportional hazards, Aalen additive hazards, and accelerated failure time (AFT) models with time-varying covariates, the difference method was compared across different data generation processes with time-dependent mediators and confounders, focusing on bias in estimated indirect effects. The parametric mediational g-formula was used as benchmark comparator. If correctly specified, the Aalen model based difference method produced unbiased estimates in the absence of a time-dependent confounder that was directly caused by the treatment. Similar results were obtained when using the Cox model based difference with rare outcomes, but not with common outcomes. The AFT model based difference method was biased in almost all scenarios, due to collapsibility issues. Only the parametric mediational g-formula was unbiased in all scenarios. In contrast to specialized methods, the difference method requires additional, often unrealistic, assumptions, such as the absence of a direct causal relationship of the treatment on time-dependent confounders. If those assumptions hold, however, it may be used as a simple and efficient alternative.

</details>


### [257] [Huber-Wasserstein barycenters for robust distribution-valued data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13131)
*Carlos Cardoso-Perelló,Alberto González-Sanz*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Huber损失直接嵌入最优传输成本的鲁棒重心（barycenter），用于分布值数据，它在局部保持二次行为的同时限制大位移的影响，并建立了完整的分析与统计理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的度量空间Huber均值是在Wasserstein距离优化完成后再施加Huber损失，鲁棒化发生在聚合层面而非逐位移层面，难以有效抵御局部形状污染并保留一阶信息。作者旨在构造一种对离群/污染分布更稳健、且能自然插值于Wasserstein均值与L1型中位数之间的重心方法。

Method: 将Huber损失直接引入最优传输成本，作用于单个传输位移（displacement-wise），构建Huber-Wasserstein重心。理论部分证明了对偶势的正则性与唯一性、最优传输映射的存在性，以及Huber参数变化时的稳定性；对重心问题证明存在性与刻画、经验plug-in估计量的一致性、有限样本崩溃点约等于1/2。一维情形下推导逐点影响函数与渐近分布，并通过数值实验验证。

Result: 在Huber最优传输中建立了对偶势正则性、唯一性、最优映射存在性与参数稳定性；Huber-Wasserstein重心具有存在性与刻画性质、经验估计量一致性，且有限样本崩溃点约为1/2。一维下推导出影响函数与渐近分布，表明逐位移Huber化可在局部形状污染下保留被基于距离的Huber化所丢失的一阶信息。数值实验证实了其鲁棒性及均值-中位数行为间的插值特性。

Conclusion: 所提出的Huber-Wasserstein重心是一项兼具理论保证与实用鲁棒性的分布值数据重心构造，能有效在均值与中位数行为之间插值，并优于基于距离的Huber化方法。

Abstract: We propose a robust barycenter for distribution-valued data by incorporating the Huber loss directly into the optimal transport cost. In contrast to metric-space Huber means, which apply the Huber loss to the Wasserstein distance after optimization, our construction acts on individual transport displacements, preserving quadratic behavior locally while limiting the influence of large displacements. The resulting Huber-Wasserstein barycenters form a natural interpolation between Wasserstein means and $L^1$-type Wasserstein medians.
  We establish the analytical and statistical foundations of this construction. For optimal transport with Huber loss, we prove regularity and uniqueness properties of dual potentials, existence of optimal transport maps, and stability as the Huber parameter varies. For the associated barycenter problem, we prove existence and characterization results, consistency of empirical plug-in estimators, and a finite-sample breakdown point essentially equal to $1/2$. In dimension one, we further derive the pointwise influence function and asymptotic distribution, quantify the associated robustness-efficiency trade-off, and show that displacement-wise Huberization can retain first-order information that is lost by distance-based Huberization under localized shape contamination. Numerical experiments on contaminated distribution-valued data demonstrate the robustness of the proposed barycenters and illustrate their interpolation between mean- and median-like behavior.

</details>


### [258] [R-estimation in a Linear Model with Autoregressive Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13150)
*Jana Jurečková,Hira Koul,Jan Picek*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文在含有一阶自回归误差扰动的线性回归模型中，提出一种基于自回归秩得分（autoregression rank scores）的非参数回归参数向量估计方法，该估计对自回归参数不变，因而对隐蔽线性趋势等结构化扰动不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经济学、水文学等应用中，回归模型误差常存在自回归结构或其他隐蔽的线性趋势与结构化扰动；传统回归参数估计容易受这些扰动影响，因此需要构造一种对可能存在的自回归误差不敏感的稳健估计方法。

Method: 在线性回归模型框架下，利用自回归秩得分（autoregression rank scores）作为主要工具，构造回归参数向量 β 的非参数估计。该估计对自回归参数具有不变性，从而在误差存在自回归时仍保持稳健。

Result: 所得的估计量对自回归参数不变，能够抵抗潜在的隐蔽线性趋势或其他结构化扰动；该方法适用于经济、水文等经常出现此类扰动的应用场景。

Conclusion: 基于自回归秩得分的非参数估计提供了一种对自回归误差扰动不敏感的回归参数估计方案，可有效应对实际数据中常见的隐蔽线性趋势与结构化扰动。

Abstract: In the linear regression model, we construct a nonparametric estimate of the regression parameter vector $\boldgreekβ$ that is insensitive to a possible nuisance autoregression in the model errors. The main tool for estimating $\boldgreekβ$ is based on the autoregression rank scores of the model. The resulting estimator is invariant to the autoregression parameters and thus remains insensitive to potential hidden linear trends or other structured disturbances, which frequently occur in economic, hydrological, and related applications.

</details>


### [259] [Chance-constrained selection of sequential intervention strategies from counterfactual estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13209)
*Minkyoung Kim,Beakcheol Jang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种“先预测后优化”的框架，用于在累积资源预算约束下进行干预序列决策。该方法通过机会约束选择，直接限制累积成本超预算的概率，而非仅约束期望成本，并给出无分布的有限样本保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多运营决策是累积资源限制下的一系列干预（如维修排程、治疗方案），需要在反事实层面权衡结果与成本。但期望成本相同的两种策略超预算的频率可能差异极大，仅约束均值无法限制超支概率。现有的先预测后优化架构或仅约束均值、或在单一决策点分配，而能约束成本尾部分布的方法又依赖预先指定的模型而非从数据中识别。因此需要一种能直接控制成本尾部、且从数据中识别反事实量、并支持多阶段决策的方法。

Method: 提出“先预测后优化”框架：(1) 预测步骤——任意能返回结果值与成本分布的估计器均可作为输入，预测器可互换；(2) 优化步骤——在有限候选集上进行机会约束选择，直接约束累积成本超过预算的概率；由于成本尾部无法跨阶段分解，每个策略被整体评分。通过扫描可容忍的违规概率可绘制“安全-效用前沿”，并给出覆盖违规概率与结果缺口的无分布有限样本界。

Result: 在五个环境中验证：其中四个（涵盖临床治疗与设备维护）提供精确的反事实真实值，第五个来自数字健康微随机试验的真实结果。在全部环境中，所提规则能在点估计规则超支的场景下守住预算，同时以显式的前沿形式呈现所付出的结果代价。

Conclusion: 该框架通过机会约束选择直接限制超预算概率，克服了仅约束均值成本无法控制尾部风险的缺陷；预测器可互换保证了灵活性，无分布有限样本界提供了理论保障。实验表明该方法能在保持预算约束的同时，以安全-效用前沿明确刻画结果上的权衡。

Abstract: Many operational decisions are sequences of interventions under a cumulative resource limit, such as a maintenance schedule within a crew-hour budget. Choosing among them calls for the outcome and the cumulative cost each would produce, counterfactual quantities identified from observational data. Two strategies with the same expected cost can exceed the budget at very different rates, so constraining the mean does not bound how often an overrun occurs. Prior two-step architectures, recently extended to continuous doses, constrain the mean cost rather than its tail and allocate at a single decision point. Methods that do bound a cost tail take its distribution from a specified model rather than identifying it from data. We present a predict-then-optimize framework. In the prediction step, any estimator returning an outcome value and a cost distribution supplies what the decision rule consumes, so the predictor is interchangeable. In the optimization step, a chance-constrained selection over a finite candidate set bounds the probability that the cumulative cost exceeds the budget. That tail does not decompose across stages, so each strategy is scored whole. Sweeping the tolerated violation probability traces a safety-utility frontier, and distribution-free finite-sample bounds cover violation and outcome shortfall. Four of five environments, spanning clinical treatment and equipment maintenance, supply exact counterfactual ground truth; the fifth carries real outcomes from a digital-health micro-randomized trial. Across them, the rule holds the budget where a point-estimate rule overruns it, at an outcome cost the frontier makes explicit. All code is available at https://github.com/mfriendly/counterfactual-chance-selection

</details>


### [260] [Causal Mediation Analysis for Network Data with Graph Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13274)
*Peikai Wu,Zhiguo Xiao*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文在单一大型观测网络下提出非参数因果中介分析框架，允许处理和中介同时存在溢出效应及高维网络混杂，使用图神经网络学习高维讨厌函数，构建了双重稳健（受控效应）和多重稳健（自然效应）的增强逆概率加权估计量，并建立渐近正态性与网络HAC方差估计，模拟与中国农业保险实验再分析验证了方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果中介分析基于无干扰假设，但网络化群体中该假设常被违背；同时需同时处理中介溢出、处理溢出和高维网络混杂，且不预设干扰机制或特定聚合模型地分离自身效应与溢出效应。

Method: 构建非参数框架，用暴露映射（exposure mapping）与中介映射（mediator mapping）定义因果估计量，分离自身效应与溢出效应；在加强的条件独立条件下识别受控直接效应、自然直接效应与自然间接效应，并给出基于结构误差的原始充分条件；采用图神经网络从节点特征和邻接矩阵学习高维讨厌函数，构造增强逆概率加权估计量；在近似邻域干扰、弱网络依赖及适当第一阶段速率下证明渐近正态性，并给出网络HAC方差估计量的一致性。

Result: 模拟中图神经网络估计量优于基于手工构造邻域特征的机器学习方法；对中国农村农业保险实验的再分析发现保险知识是实质性的中介渠道，而基于感知的中介变量不显著。

Conclusion: 该方法能在网络干扰与高维网络混杂下有效识别和估计直接与间接效应，图神经网络学习讨厌函数显著提升估计性能，实证分析证实保险知识而非感知类变量是主要中介渠道。

Abstract: Causal mediation analysis is typically formulated under no interference, an assumption often violated in networked populations. We develop a nonparametric framework for a single large observed network that allows simultaneous treatment and mediator spillovers and high-dimensional network confounding. Exposure and mediator mappings define causal estimands without restricting the true interference mechanism, separating own from spillover effects without prespecified aggregation models. Under strengthened conditional independence conditions, we identify own controlled direct, natural direct, and natural indirect effects and give primitive sufficient conditions in terms of structural errors. We construct augmented inverse probability weighted estimators that are doubly robust for controlled effects and multiply robust for natural effects, using graph neural networks to learn high-dimensional nuisance functions from node features and the adjacency matrix. Under approximate neighborhood interference, weak network dependence, and suitable first-stage rates, we establish asymptotic normality of the effect estimators and consistency of a network HAC variance estimator. In simulations the graph neural network estimator outperforms machine learning methods built on hand-constructed neighborhood features, and a reanalysis of an agricultural insurance experiment in rural China finds insurance knowledge to be a substantive mediating channel while perception-based mediators are not.

</details>


### [261] [Distributed Selective Inference for Quantile Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13311)
*Xiaohui Yuan,Jiahan Teng,Yan Zhou*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向高维分位数回归的分布式选择性推断框架，通过响应替代策略将非光滑分位数损失转化为惩罚最小二乘问题，并引入随机化程序刻画 Lasso 选择事件，从而在仅需三轮通信的条件下实现有效的选择后推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维分位数回归中选择后推断面临两大难题：一是分位数损失的非光滑性带来的计算困难，二是分布式环境下如何在保证推断有效性的同时降低通信成本。因此需要一种既能处理非光滑损失、又适合分布式计算的选择性推断方法。

Method: 采用响应替代（response-surrogation）策略，将非光滑分位数损失转化为惩罚最小二乘（penalized least-squares）形式；引入随机化程序，借助 Karush-Kuhn-Tucker 条件刻画 Lasso 选择事件；在给定选择事件的条件下推导聚合估计量的条件分布；据此构造选择性推断算法，仅需本地机器与中心服务器之间三轮通信，并用大偏差近似逼近选择性似然以实现计算可行。

Result: 在标准正则条件下证明了所提程序的渐近有效性，并给出了选择性似然的大偏差近似以保证计算可处理性。模拟研究与真实数据应用表明该方法具有令人满意的有限样本表现。

Conclusion: 所提出的分布式选择性推断框架成功解决了高维分位数回归中选择后推断的非光滑性与分布式计算难题，在保证渐近有效、通信高效（仅三轮）的同时，数值实验验证了其良好的有限样本性能，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: We propose a distributed selective inference framework tailored for high-dimensional quantile regression. To enable valid post-selection inference in this context, we address the computational challenge posed by the non-smooth quantile loss via a response-surrogation strategy. This strategy transforms the problem into a penalized least-squares formulation, thereby facilitating the application of distributed selective inference. For valid post-selection inference, a randomized procedure is introduced, in which the Lasso selection event is characterized through the associated Karush-Kuhn-Tucker conditions and the conditional distribution of the aggregated estimator is derived given the selection event. The resulting algorithm requires only three rounds of communication between local machines and the central server. Under standard regularity conditions, we establish the asymptotic validity of the proposed procedure and develop a large-deviation approximation to the selective likelihood for computationally tractable implementation. Simulation studies and a real-data application demonstrate the satisfactory finite-sample performance of the proposed method.

</details>


### [262] [Recursive Multiple Change Point Detection of Nonstationary Time Series: Instability Tests, Estimation and Confidence Intervals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13352)
*Leheng Cai,Zhou Zhou*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出BARBS方法，一种基于自助法辅助的递归二分分割，用于在一般非平稳时序动态下检测多个变点，并通过高斯乘子自助校准临界值以保证误差控制与定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有变点检测方法在复杂非平稳相依结构下的稳健性不足，亟需一种能控制第一类错误、同时具备良好定位性质的通用多变点检测方法。

Method: 提出针对CUSUM统计量的高斯乘子自助法，对递归各阶段临界值进行精细校准，构建BARBS递归二分分割算法，并引入第二阶段精细化估计量。

Result: BARBS在无变点原假设下控制第一类错误；存在变点时以预设概率识别正确变点数，变点位置估计量达到与经典二分分割一致的一致收敛速率；二阶精细化估计量达到最优个体定位速率，并获得固定与消失跳变幅度下的渐近分布与近最优一致定位速率。

Conclusion: 数值实验证实BARBS的稳健性与优越性能，应用于美国通胀数据所得的变点与多个已记录宏观经济事件相吻合，说明方法具有实用价值。

Abstract: We develop bootstrap-assisted robust binary segmentation (BARBS), a recursive binary segmentation method for multiple change point detection under general nonstationary temporal dynamics. A novel Gaussian multiplier bootstrap for the CUSUM statistics is proposed, offering robustness to complex dependence structures. Through meticulous calibration of the critical values at each stage of the recursion, BARBS ensures control of the Type I error under the null hypothesis of no change points. When change points are present, BARBS identifies the correct number of changes with a prespecified probability, and the resulting change point location estimators attain the same uniform consistency rate as classical binary segmentation. Building on this, we introduce second-stage refined estimators that achieve the optimal individual localization rate, and establish their asymptotic distributions and nearly optimal uniform localization rates under both fixed and vanishing jump magnitudes. Extensive numerical experiments across various settings confirm the robustness and superior performance of BARBS relative to existing approaches. To illustrate the practical relevance of the proposed methodology, we analyze U.S. inflation data, yielding change points that align with several documented macroeconomic episodes.

</details>


### [263] [Weighted cumulative past inaccuracy and Kullback-Leibler divergence based on extropy: properties, estimation, and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13363)
*Bighneswar Sahoo,Suchandan Kayal*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种加权框架，利用累积过去剩余熵（cumulative past extropy）度量两个非负寿命分布之间的差异，包括加权累积过去剩余熵不精确度（WCPEI）与加权累积过去剩余熵KL散度（WCPED），并推广至动态版本，配套给出非参数估计及仿真与两个实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于熵/剩余熵的分布差异度量多采用均匀或单一权重，无法灵活反映观测值重要性；同时缺乏在动态（条件于生存至今的信息）场景下对过去寿命分布差异的度量工具。因此需要一种带广义权重的、静态与动态兼容的框架，以更精准地刻画非负寿命分布之间的偏差。

Method: 在累积过去剩余熵基础上引入广义权重函数，构造两个度量：加权累积过去剩余熵不精确度（WCPEI）与加权累积过去剩余熵KL散度（WCPED），并推导其理论性质。基于经验分布函数构造WCPEI比值（WCPEIR）的非参数估计量，通过蒙特卡洛仿真考察其有限样本表现。进一步将框架推广至动态设定，得到DWCPEI与DWCPED，推导理论性质并给出非参数估计方法。

Result: 给出WCPEI、WCPED及其动态版本DWCPEI、DWCPED的理论性质与估计方法；仿真验证了估计量在不同样本量下的有限样本性能；在拟合优度检验中，基于WCPED的均匀性检验在多种备择假设下具有良好功效，并可与既有均匀性检验相比；在图像分析应用中，该度量能有效评估分辨率改变时像素强度分布的变化。

Conclusion: 所提出的加权累积过去剩余熵框架为度量非负寿命分布差异提供了灵活、有效的工具，兼具静态与动态版本，理论性质完备，非参数估计在小样本下表现良好，且在均匀性检验与图像分析两个应用中展现出实际价值。

Abstract: This study develops a weighted framework for measuring the discrepancy between two nonnegative lifetime distributions through cumulative past extropy. We propose two measures, referred to as the weighted cumulative past extropy inaccuracy (WCPEI) and the weighted cumulative past extropy Kullback-Leibler divergence (WCPED). The generalized weight function is considered in this study. We investigate a number of theoretical properties of these measures. Empirical distribution function-based nonparametric estimator is subsequently constructed for the weighted cumulative past extropy inaccuracy ration (WCPEIR). Its finite-sample behavior is studied through Monte Carlo simulation experiments for different sample sizes. To illustrate the practical relevance of the WCPED, two applications are considered. First, an extropy-based goodness-of-fit procedure for testing uniformity is developed using the proposed divergence measure. Its power is then compared with that of several established uniformity tests under a variety of alternatives. Second, an image analysis application is presented in which the proposed measure is employed to assess changes in the distributions of pixel intensities when the image resolution is altered. The framework is further extended to a dynamic setting by conditioning on the lifetime information available up to a specified time point. This leads to the dynamic weighted cumulative past extropy inaccuracy (DWCPEI) and dynamic weighted cumulative past extropy divergence (DWCPED). Their theoretical properties are derived, and the corresponding nonparametric estimation procedures are proposed. The finite-sample performance of these estimators is evaluated through simulation studies using R software.

</details>


### [264] [COBRA-DOSE: Copula-based Bayesian Model Averaging for Dose Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13423)
*Luke Hagar,Min Zhang,Ranjeny Thomas,Andrew J. Martin*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: COBRA-DOSE是一种基于双终点的贝叶斯后验预测推断框架，通过copula建模依赖关系，并用贝叶斯模型平均同时纳入边际分布与依赖结构的不确定性，为早期剂量选择试验（如类风湿关节炎的DEN-181 I期试验）提供跨剂量生物标志物组合结果概率的透明摘要，已实现为R包CobraDose。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期剂量选择临床试验样本量小，现代靶向疗法/免疫学试验常需寻找"最佳生物剂量"（即安全前提下达到足够生物活性的最低剂量），而该评估依赖多个生物标志物或临床终点。临床医生需要跨剂量观察生物标志物组合结果概率的透明摘要，但在小样本下复杂建模假设难以验证，这构成了核心挑战。

Method: 提出COBRA-DOSE框架：基于两个终点进行后验预测推断，利用copula对终点间的依赖关系建模，并通过贝叶斯模型平均（BMA）同时纳入边际分布和依赖结构的不确定性，避免依赖单一模型，生成可解释的临床决策量；通过R包CobraDose提供通用实现。

Result: 在DEN-181（一项针对类风湿关节炎的I期免疫学试验）上验证了COBRA-DOSE的性能，展示了其在真实早期剂量选择场景中的应用效果。

Conclusion: COBRA-DOSE为小样本早期剂量选择提供了稳健、透明且可解释的推断工具，不依赖单一模型假设，能有效支持基于多终点生物活性的最佳生物剂量确定，并通过开源R包便于实际推广使用。

Abstract: Early-phase clinical trials for dose selection typically enrol few patients and aim to identify doses that are both safe and promising for further study. While traditional approaches identify the maximum tolerated dose, modern trials for targeted therapies often seek the optimal biological dose, defined as the lowest dose achieving sufficient biological activity with acceptable safety. In immunology settings, assessment of biological activity is based on multiple biomarkers or clinical endpoints. Clinicians leading dose-selection efforts would thus benefit from transparent summaries of the probabilities of observing combinations of biomarker outcomes across doses. However, such inference is challenging in small samples where complex modelling assumptions are difficult to verify. To address this limitation, we propose COBRA-DOSE, a framework for posterior predictive inference based on two endpoints that models dependence via copulas and accounts for uncertainty in both marginal distributions and dependence structures through Bayesian model averaging. This approach avoids reliance on a single model and yields interpretable quantities for clinical decision making. We demonstrate the performance of COBRA-DOSE using DEN-181, a phase I immunology trial in rheumatoid arthritis. We also provide a general implementation of our approach through the CobraDose package in R.

</details>


### [265] [Retrospective Statistical Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13439)
*Chong Gu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的统计推断范式：以观测数据的点估计为“真值”生成模拟副本（克隆），并基于克隆分布进行推断。该方法避免前瞻性的有限维模型假设，适用于非参数函数估计，并在参数回归中与传统推断在操作上高度相似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统统计推断依赖前瞻性的有限维模型假设，难以推广到非参数函数估计等复杂场景；作者希望建立一种不依赖此类模型假设、也不对真值作概率论断的替代性不确定性量化体系。

Method: 以观测数据所得的点估计为中心，将该估计视为“真值”来模拟生成复制数据，从而得到估计量的“克隆”分布，并据此进行推断。文中以非参数回归中的平滑样条ANOVA模型为例进行演示，并说明该方法同样适用于参数回归。

Result: 所提克隆推断范式在非参数函数估计中可操作；在参数回归场景下，其推断结果与传统推断在操作层面高度接近，表明该范式具有广泛的适用性与连贯性。

Conclusion: 该范式提供了一种新的不确定性量化思路，绕开有限维模型假设的限制，适用于非参数与参数推断；全文贯穿概念性讨论，论证其作为一种替代推断体系的潜力和意义。

Abstract: In this article, we explore a new paradigm for statistical inference. The approach centers around the point estimate based on observed data, simulating replicates using the estimate as the truth to produce clones of the estimate, with inference deriving from the clone distribution. It avoids prospective finite-dimensional model assumptions, but it makes no probabilistic claims concerning the truth; it suggests an alternative system of uncertainty quantification that is operable in nonparametric function estimation. The procedures are demonstrated using examples of smoothing spline ANOVA models in nonparametric regression. The paradigm also applies in parametric regression, where the proposed inference closely resembles traditional inference operation-wise. Conceptual discussions are scattered throughout.

</details>


### [266] [Theoretical Properties of Covariate-Adaptive Randomization with a Diverging Number of Covariates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13442)
*Yuhang Tao,Li-Xin Zhang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究在高维（协变量数量发散）设定下两类统一的协变量自适应随机化程序的理论性质，建立指定协变量不平衡度量的收敛速率，并针对其中一类推导未指定协变量的渐近性质，进而得出平均处理效应的差值均值估计量的渐近性质并构造渐近95%置信区间，辅以数值与实证研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协变量自适应随机化在临床试验中被广泛用于改善协变量平衡，而现代应用中实验者常可获取大量协变量，因此迫切需要建立协变量数量发散（高维）情形下协变量自适应随机化程序的理论。

Method: 在高维设定下研究两族统一的协变量自适应随机化程序，建立其指定协变量不平衡度量的收敛速率；对其中一类程序进一步分析未指定协变量不平衡的渐近性质，并据此推导平均处理效应差值均值估计量的渐近分布以构造置信区间。

Result: 为两类程序建立了对应指定协变量的不平衡度量的收敛速率；对其中一类得到未指定协变量不平衡的渐近性质，并由此导出平均处理效应的差值均值估计量的渐近性质，构造出渐近95%置信区间。

Conclusion: 本文为协变量数量发散情形下的协变量自适应随机化提供了理论支撑，数值与实证研究验证了理论结果的实用价值，有助于在高维协变量场景中更好地设计与分析临床试验。

Abstract: Covariate-adaptive randomization procedures are widely used in clinical trials to improve covariate balance. In modern applications, experimenters often have access to many covariates, motivating the need for a theory of covariate-adaptive randomization procedures with a diverging number of covariates. In this paper, we study the theoretical properties of two unified families of covariate-adaptive randomization procedures under high-dimensional settings. For the two procedures, we establish the convergence rate of the imbalance measure corresponding to the specified covariates. In addition, for one of them, we study the asymptotic properties of the imbalance of unspecified covariates under and apply these results to derive the asymptotic properties of the difference-in-means estimator for the average treatment effect and construct asymptotic 95% confidence intervals. Furthermore, we provide extensive numerical and empirical studies to illustrate the practical relevance of our theoretical results.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [267] [Black-Box Knowledge Transfer across Distinct Feature Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12403)
*Oh-Ran Kwon,Daeyoung Ham*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将预训练黑箱预测函数的知识迁移到异构（不同）输入空间的方法，通过分解目标回归函数为可迁移与不可迁移两部分，利用跨空间特征对和少量标签进行两步神经网络估计，并给出优于非迁移方法的预测风险界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练黑箱模型蕴含由海量数据和计算蒸馏出的知识，但当新任务的输入特征与黑箱所期望的特征不一致（异构输入空间）时，无法直接使用该黑箱。现有方法缺乏在输入维度/特征不同的情况下有效利用黑箱知识的机制，因此需要一种能把黑箱预测知识迁移到新的异构输入空间的方法。

Method: 将目标回归函数分解为“可迁移分量”（可由黑箱提供信息）与“不可迁移分量”（捕捉新空间独有信息）。提出两步神经网络估计流程：第一步利用大量无标签的、连接两个输入空间的特征对估计可迁移分量；第二步利用有限标签估计不可迁移分量。并推导预测风险界，且将该框架扩展至多个各自拥有不同输入空间的黑箱，通过聚合来降低预测误差。

Result: 当不可迁移分量较小或较光滑时，所提方法的预测风险界优于非迁移（仅用标签）的替代方法，且该方法能自适应于这两种情形。在额外条件下，估计器的最坏情况风险的多项式阶数严格小于仅用标签数据估计的极小极大风险。扩展实验中多个黑箱聚合可相对单一最优黑箱进一步降低预测误差，模拟与真实数据均验证了方法的实用价值。

Conclusion: 该迁移框架能够在异构输入空间下有效复用黑箱知识，通过可迁移/不可迁移分解与两步估计，在理论上获得优于非迁移方法的预测风险保证，并可通过多黑箱聚合进一步提升性能，具有理论与实用双重价值。

Abstract: Pre-trained black-box predictive functions encode knowledge distilled from massive datasets and extensive computation. However, when the available input features differ from those the black box expects, direct use is infeasible. We introduce a method for transferring predictive knowledge from the black box to a new, heterogeneous input space. Our approach decomposes the target regression function into a transferable component, which the black box can inform, and a non-transferable component, which captures information unique to the new space. We propose a two-step neural network procedure, estimating the transferable component from abundant unlabeled feature pairs that bridge the two input spaces and the non-transferable component from limited labels. We derive prediction risk bounds that improve on those of a non-transfer alternative when the non-transferable component is small or smooth, and the procedure adapts to either case. Under additional conditions, the worst-case risk of our estimator is of strictly smaller polynomial order than the minimax risk of estimation from the labeled data alone. We extend the framework to multiple black boxes, each on its own input space, and show that aggregation can reduce prediction error relative to the best single black box. Simulated and real data demonstrate the practical value of the method.

</details>


### [268] [SSPO: Structure-Aware Similarity-Weighted Preference Optimization for Neural Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12443)
*Yuanyu Li,Jintao Xu,Zijiang Liu,Yongzhi Qi,Ningxuan Kang,Jianshen Zhang,Wei Qi,Chen Xie,Zuo-Jun Max Shen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出 SSPO（结构感知相似度加权的偏好优化）方法，用于改进神经组合优化（NCO）训练。核心思路是通过一种基于不相似度加权的留一法基线，联合利用一组并行采样的所有解的信息，同时解决现有偏好优化方法"梯度信号极化"和均值基线"基线冗余"两大问题，并在多个基准（TSP、EFL、JSP）上取得一致提升，且已在京东生产系统中部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 NCO 训练依赖并行解采样，但未充分利用同组采样解的丰富信息。偏好优化方法只锚定单一最优解、丢弃其他解的质量与结构信号，导致"梯度信号极化"；基于均值的基线对所有解等权处理，使结构近乎相同的解向基线灌入冗余信息、抬高梯度方差，导致"基线冗余"。因此需要一种机制同时解决这两种失效模式。

Method: 提出 SSPO：通过不相似度加权的留一法（leave-one-out）基线，对所有 B 个采样解进行联合评分。结构上差异更大的解获得更高权重，从而在一个机制内同时解决极化与冗余问题。该基线使用零参数、问题自适应的解嵌入，嵌入直接由编码器已有的节点表示构建，无需额外可学习参数。

Result: 在 TSP、EFL、JSP 基准上，SSPO 相比此前的最优锚点基线与等权基线均取得一致提升。与 TSP 和 EFL 上的均匀 RLOO 直接对比证实，结构感知加权是性能提升的主要驱动因素。SSPO 训练的 EFL 策略已部署于京东生产设施选址系统，验证了大规模实际可行性。

Conclusion: SSPO 通过结构感知的相似度加权留一法基线，统一解决了偏好优化中的梯度信号极化和均值基线中的基线冗余问题，无需额外参数即可利用同组采样解的丰富结构信息，在多个组合优化基准上稳定提升性能，并在真实生产环境中证明其规模化实用价值。

Abstract: Neural combinatorial optimization (NCO) relies on parallel solution sampling for training, yet existing methods fail to fully exploit the rich information latent in a co-sampled solution group. Preference-optimization methods anchor on the single best solution and discard fine-grained quality and structural signal from all other peers-a failure we term gradient signal polarization. Mean-based baselines instead weight peers uniformly, so structurally near-identical peers flood the baseline with redundant information and keep gradient variance high-a failure we term baseline redundancy. We propose SSPO (Structure-Aware Similarity-Weighted Preference Optimization), which scores all $B$ sampled solutions jointly through a dissimilarity-weighted leave-one-out baseline: structurally distinct peers receive higher weight, resolving both failures in a single mechanism. The baseline uses zero-parameter, problem-adaptive solution embeddings built from the encoder's existing node representations. Experiments on TSP, EFL, and JSP benchmarks show consistent gains over prior best-anchor and uniform-weight baselines. A direct comparison against uniform RLOO on TSP and EFL confirms that structure-aware weighting is the primary driver of improvement. The SSPO-trained EFL policy has been deployed in a production facility-location system at JD$\mathord{.}$com, confirming practical viability at scale.

</details>


### [269] [Online Inference for Quantile Temporal Difference Learning in Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12973)
*Zijie Cheng,Yang Peng,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究分布强化学习中分位时序差分学习（QTD）的统计推断问题，在生成模型假设下建立同步与异步 QTD 的函数中心极限定理，并提出基于随机缩放的在线推断方法，构造渐近枢轴统计量以降低内存需求并实现高效推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布强化学习中的 QTD 算法缺乏系统的统计推断工具，现有方法难以对平均迭代结果的渐近分布进行刻画，且传统推断需要存储完整迭代轨迹，内存开销大、难以在线实现，因此需要建立理论极限定理并设计低内存的在线推断方法。

Method: 在可访问生成模型的假设下，首先对同步和异步 QTD 建立函数中心极限定理，证明其平均迭代弱收敛于重标度布朗运动；随后基于随机缩放构造渐近枢轴统计量，利用整条 QTD 路径信息实现在线推断，且无需存储完整迭代轨迹。

Result: 同步与异步 QTD 的平均迭代均弱收敛于重标度布朗运动，得到函数中心极限定理；所提在线推断统计量渐近服从枢轴分布，可在不存储完整轨迹的情况下在线计算，显著降低内存需求。

Conclusion: 本文为分布强化学习的 QTD 算法建立了严格的渐近理论与实用的在线统计推断框架，通过函数中心极限定理和随机缩放统计量，实现了低内存、高效率的在线推断，为分布强化学习的统计分析奠定了基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we study how to perform statistical inference for quantile temporal difference learning (QTD) in distributional reinforcement learning. Assuming access to a generative model, we first establish functional central limit theorems for both synchronous and asynchronous QTD, which show that the averaged iterates of QTD converge weakly to a rescaled Brownian motion. We next provide online inference methods. Based on random scaling, the inference procedure constructs an asymptotically pivotal statistic for inference by using the information along the whole QTD path. Meanwhile, the proposed statistic can be computed online without storing the entire trajectory of QTD iterates. This substantially reduces the memory requirement and enables efficient statistical inference in distributional reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [270] [Statistical Properties of Robust Learning under Distributional Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13133)
*Zhiyi Li,Xiaojie Mao,Yunbei Xu,Ruohan Zhan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究分布漂移下DRO与RS两种鲁棒学习框架在目标环境的有限样本泛化误差界，提出信息导向的超参数校准方法并系统比较二者，最后以网络批量订购问题实证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布漂移普遍存在于部署环境与训练环境不一致的现实中。DRO和RS虽旨在应对该挑战，但现有分析通常只在源环境建立保证，或只针对模糊集上的对抗最坏情形，缺乏在目标环境（漂移后分布）下的有限样本保证，也缺少对两种方法的系统比较。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 首先，在漂移后的目标环境下，为DRO和RS分别推导有限样本泛化误差界，显式刻画“对漂移的敏感度降低”与“鲁棒超参数带来的正则化惩罚”之间的权衡，并避免Wasserstein经验集中的维度灾难。其次，在可获得部分漂移信息（如漂移幅度或方向）时，提出信息导向的超参数校准方法，并在相同信息条件下比较两种方法。最后，将框架应用于网络批量订购问题，解读鲁棒策略对需求分布正向漂移的响应。

Result: 推导出的界显式展示了敏感度与正则化惩罚的权衡且规避维度灾难。在同等部分信息及相应校准下，DRO与RS在本文研究的部分信息机制中表现出互补的理论与实证行为。网络批量订购应用中，框架能够解释鲁棒策略对需求正向漂移的响应方式。

Conclusion: 结果填补了鲁棒学习方法在分布漂移下统计性质的理解空白，并为比较DRO与RS提供了原理性依据。DRO与RS在不同部分信息机制下各有优势，表现为互补关系，为实际选择鲁棒方法及超参数校准提供了指导。

Abstract: Distributional shifts arise when the target deployment environment differs from the source environment that generated the training data. Robust learning frameworks such as Distributionally Robust Optimization (DRO) and Robust Satisficing (RS) aim to address this challenge, yet their finite-sample guarantees under such shifts, and their systematic comparison, remain underexplored: existing analyses typically establish guarantees either in the source environment or for adversarial worst-case performance over an ambiguity set. This paper instead studies generalization error in the target environment---the excess loss under the shifted target distribution. Our contributions are threefold. First, we derive finite-sample generalization error bounds in the shifted target environment for both DRO and RS. These bounds explicitly characterize the trade-off between reduced sensitivity to shift and the regularization penalty induced by each method's robustness hyperparameter, and they avoid the curse of dimensionality associated with Wasserstein empirical concentration. Second, when partial shift information such as shift magnitude or direction is available, we propose information-directed hyperparameter calibrations and compare the two methods given the same information. Under these calibrations, and in the partial-information regimes we study, DRO and RS exhibit complementary theoretical and empirical behavior. Finally, we apply the framework to a network lot-sizing problem, using it to interpret how robust policies respond to positive shifts in the demand distribution. Together, these results fill a gap in understanding the statistical properties of robust learning methods under distributional shifts and provide a principled basis for comparing DRO and RS.

</details>


### [271] [High-dimensional networks and mean squared error for possibly misspecified models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13171)
*Lourens Waldorp*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 在高维网络分析中（参数远多于观测），本文证明最小描述长度（MDL）方法能以低假阳性率正确（或偏保守地）选择每个节点的邻域，而Lasso、AIC、BIC等方法倾向于产生虚假边。关键在于惩罚项需纳入模型空间的体积，并揭示了岭参数对均方误差及“双下降”现象的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络分析中纳入的变量越来越多，导致高维设定（参数远多于观测数）。传统模型选择方法（Lasso、AIC、BIC）在此设定下易产生虚假边（高假阳性率），因此需要一种邻域选择方法，能在真实模型为线性或非线性两种情况下都实现低假阳性率的正确（或保守）估计。

Method: 作者将邻域估计建模为线性模型（即每个节点的连接通过线性模型估计），区分两种情况：真实模型为线性时邻域选择表现尚可；真实模型为非线性时需要对高维进行惩罚。他们分析了岭参数对均方误差的影响，说明其能降低测试方差从而产生大量边，并将此与机器学习中的“双下降”现象相关联。核心结论是：在大参数模型下，惩罚项必须纳入模型空间的体积（volume of the model space）。作者证明在高维设定下，最小描述长度（MDL）在模型正确设定为线性或错误假定为线性两种情况下，都能实现正确或偏小（低假阳性率）的邻域选择。

Result: 大多数邻域选择方法（如Lasso、AIC、BIC）会产生虚假边（高假阳性率）；而最小描述长度（MDL）在高维设置下，无论是模型被正确假定为线性还是被错误假定为线性，都能得到正确或更小的邻域（低假阳性率）。岭参数影响均方误差并降低测试方差，与机器学习中的“双下降”现象相关联。

Conclusion: 在高维网络邻域选择中，适当的惩罚必须包含模型空间的体积。相较于Lasso、AIC、BIC等易产生虚假边的方法，最小描述长度（MDL）能在模型正确或错误假定为线性的情况下均表现出低假阳性率，是更可靠的邻域选择方法。

Abstract: To avoid missing important variables and their connections in networks, more and more variables are included in network analysis.
